Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on spin structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 977. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 37 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to spin structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 977/37 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in spin theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 723 and the invariant I(A,B) = 412 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 723 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spin structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 412. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
